Root cause (in-process t1mix import with `--cpu-prof` and an event-loop probe, `node:perf_hooks`): 45 of 48 s CPU in synchronous SQLite commits; the parse-commit loop over one 1 MiB read chunk was a microtask chain, so a 5 ms timer ran p95 97 / p99 200 / max 707 ms late; Codex and agy watchers did not exist during the Claude backfill (adapters started one after another); settle was detected on the 250 ms tick. Variants measured (yield only; 25 or 50 or 10 rows; 128 KiB or 256 KiB or 64 KiB; read chunk 128 KiB; with or without an immediate flush; Node "Don't block the event loop" guidance): chosen 25 rows / 128 KiB / 128 KiB chunks + immediate flush + `setImmediate` after each commit (p95 ~20 / p99 ~27 / max ~73 ms, import ~51 s); 25 rows without the immediate flush took ~451 s (the 50 ms coalescing timer per batch). Change: `tail-adapter.ts` (defaults, flush every full batch, yield, `watch()`/`backfill()`), `boot.ts` (every watcher before any backfill; `onTick` with one timer at the earliest watched `settleAt`, incremental per watch, rescan on the tick and the timer), `status/engine.ts` (`settleAt`), `index.ts` (pushes `onTick` keys). RED 174 (4 failures for their intended reasons) → GREEN 176 (863/863; run 175 failed only the oversized-batch test, which did not reach the oversized path; corrected to force it and bound FTS content per batch, checked failing at the old 1 MiB settings: 600 KiB vs 196 KiB). Review (ecc:code-reviewer, sonnet, fresh context, diff-only): approve-with-fixes, no critical/high; M1 (a full rescan per watch: 5,000 rows ~1.8–3.9 s) fixed RED-first (RED 177 → GREEN 178, 864/864); M2 (0 ms timer loop with a frozen clock) not possible (Finishing… holds only while clock < settleAt, so the delay is ≥ 2 ms); L3 (re-emitted rows after a crash mid oversized line) covered by the existing restart tests (kill/restart mid-backfill, EC-15 restart-safe: no duplicates); L4 settle test bound loosened to 200 ms. `test:electron` 355/355; E2E 20/20 on the rebuilt package; typecheck, lint, `privacy:scan` pass. Packaged re-measurement (Docker stopped): see §7 M1, M2, M3 (all pass). Probe fixes (test only): the import probe's clone keeps timestamps; spooled hooks count as misses in its M1 figure. **Codex review** (`codex exec -s read-only`, diff-only; owner request; two plugin runs stalled before reporting and a first direct run waited on stdin): approve-with-fixes, no critical/high; M1 (a record below the 8 MiB parse cap still commits as one record) measured, accepted as a limit: one valid line holds the loop ~7 ms at 1 MiB, ~24 ms at 3 MiB, ~43 ms at 6 MiB (~142 ms under the parallel suite); lowering the cap would move 1–8 MiB lines to the oversized path, which keeps only small fields (usage and tool rows lost); a hook arriving during such a commit may spool and is replayed; upgrade path: a writer worker thread if real stores show such lines during live work; guarded by a load-tolerant regression test (< 400 ms); L2 (the settle test could lose its window to a slow boot) fixed: a status clock frozen through boot, plus tests for an earlier deadline moving the timer and for no tick after `stop()` with a timer pending. Design security review (ecc:security-reviewer, opus, fresh context) approve-with-fixes before code; adopted H1 (intake refuses a suppressed session before publish or chunk write), H2 (backups via VACUUM INTO), H3 (pending file work recorded in the marker, redone at boot), M1, M4, M5, L1, L2. RED 490 → GREEN 491; boot wiring RED 492 → GREEN 493. Done: migration 022 `suppressions` (identity, reason manual/retention, at, pending registration ids); `src/service/history/purge.ts` `purgeSession(writer, id, {dataDir, backupDir, secret, now, reason})`: one writer transaction settles (FR-52), removes FTS rows with their indexed prefix, deletes the session's rows (events, turns, tool calls, usage, cost, context/usage snapshots, session-linked quota scopes and history, cwd history, subagents, links, quarantine of its files, report content/deliveries, hook deliveries/gaps, status evidence incl. legacy profile '', overlays), keeps `source_files` rows with session_id NULL (the native file name is a rescan checkpoint, not Xaibo content), writes the marker; after COMMIT only: removes chunk items (`removeItems`), rewrites closed spool files (past UTC dates, taken replay files; quiet 5 s on mtime/ctime; inode/size re-check), deletes backups holding the session and any partial backup (Q-5.1: `backupsDeleted` is the notice), clears pending, incremental_vacuum best effort; `resumePurges` (boot, off readiness). | P5-T16 follow-up (review M3: Clear usage history scan off the service thread) | done (run 157) | XAIBO-PROD-526, new-feature run 157, risk 186 medium. Owner decision 2026-11-19: move it to a worker. RED 552 -> GREEN 553. `clear.ts`: `clearKeys` (every retained contribution key not yet settled, with month and offset), `applyClear` (one writer transaction: frozen tables deleted, settled keys flagged cleared, listed keys inserted cleared), `keysInWorker` (`clearKeys` in a worker thread on its own read-only connection in one snapshot; fixed-code rejection, 120 s deadline), `clearUsageHistory` kept as the synchronous reference; `Service.clearUsage` async (worker, then one transaction; a stop meanwhile = `failed`, nothing written); `index.ts` replies when it settles. A key settled between the snapshot and the transaction is caught by the UPDATE; a contribution first stored after the snapshot counts; never chunked. Measured on synthetic data: the scan (~0.8 s per 200k records) now runs in the worker; the service thread keeps the index inserts (~0.75 s per 200k new keys; one `json_each` INSERT measured no faster), so the block is about halved, not gone; removing it needs a different marking scheme (row-id watermarks), noted in `clear.ts`. Code review (ecc:code-reviewer, sonnet, fresh, diff-only) approve; adopted RED-first (RED 554 -> GREEN 555, `npm test` 1408/1408): M1 boot-level test (`service-boot.test.ts`: async path, overlapping calls, stop mid-clear), L2 overlapping calls share one run, L4/L5 comments; accepted L3 (a stop does not terminate the worker; its result is dropped) and L6 (timing-based worker tests). Electron runtime 25/25 for the touched files; packaged `usage-clear` E2E passes; typecheck and lint clean. Implemented directly (disclosed) | 2026-11-19 |

| Phase 6 unit: post-disk-full commit latency (D-P5-20) | done: closed with its diagnosis, no product fix (D-P6-03) | Codex round 1 (read-only, companion run outside the sandbox: its job log lives under the plugin state dir): ranked synchronous store work, replay amplification, the filled image, failed-file retries; `--no-disk-full` drops the image too, so a control was needed; M1/M2 are emit->paint, not commit latency. Measured (`da894c8`, perf harness only: phase hops, `--image-only`, per-process M5; head `ab792e2` unpackaged, t1mix, seed 1, 600 s each, `.cache/p6-d520/`): steady p95 M1/M2/ingest lag: no image 154/133/60 ms, image never filled 249/392/232 ms, filled and freed 13.5 s/3.2 s/4.0 s. Fault phases: post-free (88-300 s) hook->commit p95 17.3 s, file->commit 4.95 s, commit->delta and delta->paint normal; after the service restart on the same freed volume all healthy (hook->commit 90 ms). Backfill sweep returned at ~75 s; WAL 8 MiB; 547 hooks past the forwarder deadline (190 image-only), p50 9.8 s. So not the volume and not a checkpoint backlog: state inside the running service until restart. Codex round 2: rank 1 failed-file retries left to the 5 s reconciliation scan after `backfill_done` (the sweep returns with per-file failures), rank 2 status-engine pinning (`unstored` evidence never cleared on this path, refolds), then connection/checkpoint, replay backoff (M1 amplifier), latches (low). Next: two content-free src/ instrumentation units (recovery-work counters: watch vs scan syncs, sync backlog, failed batches, replay passes; stage durations: SQL vs onCommit/status feed, timer lateness, pinned/cached sessions, refolds), and 10 s windows in the harness, then a re-run; a fix only after attribution Round 3 (counters `acb340d`, run 186; repackaged: the perf harness runs the packaged app; `.cache/p6-d520-r2`, `-r3`): three fault runs on one binary give M1 p95 13.5 s, 76 s, 2.0 s; 10 s slices show a backlog that builds and drains, and live latency is back to normal when the backfill sweep finishes (282-307 s after a fault vs 68-75 s without); the service spends ~3.7-5.0 s of each 5 s in SQL while backfill runs (~50 ms after); commits are 3-10x slower on the HFS+ image even before the fill (max 95-527 ms vs 28-50 ms); the fault adds 1443 failed syncs and a 336-sync backlog; statusStoreFailed 0. 'Healthy after the restart' only meant nothing heavy was left to write. Codex round 3: root cause = transcript ingestion saturating the single service thread (synchronous commits; hooks cannot interrupt a running transaction), lengthened by the image and the fault's re-work; spooled hooks are duplicates of deliveries that still committed (replay is not the delay; do not shorten it). Proposed fix (pending owner decision, D-P6 row): a shared per-Writer ingestion duty cycle (after each transcript commit, others wait ~min(3 x commit time, 250 ms); commitLive bypasses; atomic reservation; order and checkpoints kept), deterministic RED test without an image; cost: longer imports | — |
| Phase 6 unit: renderer and GPU memory under load (D-P5-22) | done: closed with its diagnosis, no product fix (D-P6-04) | Measured with the per-process M5 sampler (same runs as D-P5-20): M5 under load misses without any disk fault (no image: peak 486 MiB at 33 s, budget ~429 MiB). At the peak: GPU ~167, service (Utility) 152 (76 at 12 s: grows during backfill), renderer (Tab) ~118, Browser ~52; ~430 steady until the kill, ~400 after the restart. So the peak is the service during backfill on a ~150-170 MiB GPU baseline, not renderer growth (the D-P5-22 wording is revised). Codex round 2: target service backfill allocation first (status evidence cache, ingestion buffers, SQLite/FTS native memory), keep GPU measured; next measurement: service PID heapUsed/heapTotal/external/arrayBuffers with status-cache counts and one GC after import quiesces; sampler by PID, not type (the split is approximate). The Gate 4 ~50 MiB comparison needs a re-run with the same harness before it is quoted again Round 3: the service peak's JS heap stays 15-50 MiB (external 7-39), so the footprint is native; Codex: not yet attributed to SQLite (cache_size/mmap_size unset: read effective values; temp_store=MEMORY explicit, db.ts:50; FTS insert/merge); next a diagnostic A/B with temp_store=FILE only (not a shipped setting); GPU ~170-210 MiB baseline is in measurement scope, no GPU fix justified | — |

Renderer tracking (page crash/close, render-process-gone, unresponsive) added to the report; close() now kills a lingering app; the M4 sample is bounded. A 300 s rerun with that tracking lost its report: the new close() line read `app.process()` after Playwright disposed it, and the throw skipped the image detach (detached by hand); fixed (handle taken before close), not yet rerun, so whether the stall reproduces is still unknown. Reruns with that tracking: run D (600 s, disk-full) no stall (renderer: no crash, hang or close; banner shown, workspace re-opened) but slow (M1 p95 874 ms, M2 1.27 s, ingest lag 1.06 s, backfill 170 s, 17 steady hooks lost as disk-full gaps about 15 s after the window); run E (600 s, disk-full, volume free space tracked): **all checks pass**: M1 steady p95 243 ms (n 1,893), M2 346 ms, drain 109 ms, ingest lag p95 204 ms, 11,244 records once, 2,015 hooks stored + 50 disk-full gaps = sent, banner shown and cleared, workspace re-opened, renderer quiet, tee added p95 84 ms, 0 extra bytes, hook added p95 92 ms, M5 399 MiB, every message-scope usage row priced, 0 prohibited calls, backfill 122 s; the volume never fell below 1.58 GB free outside the fault (store 1.87 GB, WAL 10 MiB), so the store does not refill it; disk_full flaps full/ok three times inside the 20 s hold. Reading: on the sparse image M1 varies run to run (243 / 256 / 874 ms) with its write cost; on the normal disk the same usage load gives 173 ms. | P4-T10b (status-engine session cache: load on demand, bounded LRU) | done (run 100) | XAIBO-PROD-425, new-feature run 100, risk high (keyword "session"; security review required). `src/service/status/engine.ts` no longer folds every session's `status_evidence` at start (the hook `seq` counter comes from `max(seq) WHERE rank = 0`): a session loads from the store on first `status` or `ingest` into a use-ordered LRU of at most `maxSessions` (default `MAX_SESSIONS` 500, ponytail), trimmed after each public call. Status is a pure function of the stored evidence, so a reload refolds to the same status, dedup and producer tops, and never notifies. A session is never evicted while it holds unstored evidence (a failed insert, or one ignored by the store's unique key, which spans profiles) or while its last read was busy or heuristic (boot reads those every tick). `cached()` added to `StatusEngine` for P4-T10c. Deviation: the plan says ended sessions; the LRU also bounds sessions that never end (silence never ends a session) and keeps recently read ended ones, so a list does not reload them per read. No `live/state.ts` (the engine is the live-state map). RED 320 -> GREEN 321. Security review (ecc:security-reviewer, opus, fresh, diff-only): 0 critical/high; M1 more time-bound sessions than the cap would reload every one on each boot tick: fixed RED 322 -> GREEN 323 (`npm test` 1103/1103); accepted L1 pinned sessions grow past the cap and each trim walks them (ponytail; a hostile hook client could already grow memory before), L2 reads of sessions with no evidence run one indexed query each (uncached), L3 `maxSessions` not validated (internal option). small fields kept up to 4 KiB; a syntax error is an `oversized-unreadable` quarantine row plus file status `oversized`, content read before it stays; (5) chunk `source_seq` is exact only to one input piece (ponytail note); | Gate 3 (Claude, from an EC-46 failure under load): the forwarder arms its 60 ms `finish` deadline at module start, but the spool fallback (`unsettled`) is armed only after stdin is read; if startup stalls past 60 ms (a loaded machine), `finish` exits 0 with nothing spooled and no gap, so that hook is lost silently. Pre-existing since P3-T03 (a review comment already names an "EC-46 flake"); Codex did not flag it. Options: on the deadline, spool whatever stdin holds as a truncated envelope (evidence of a missed hook), or start the deadline when stdin is read (costs M16 budget under load), or accept as rare. Cause (code reading): `boot.ts` starts adapters one after another, so the Codex and agy watchers do not exist during the Claude backfill, and the backfill's synchronous reads, parses and commits starve the socket acks and watch callbacks. ## 9a. Plan challenge record (Codex, 2026-11-09, plan v0.1 → v0.2)

Baseline `master` at `e6b39ab`; diff `master...feat/phase0-spikes`. Lens verdicts: traceability "yes with fixes"; sequencing "no"; safety/privacy "no"; verification/consistency "no".

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition in v0.2 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | blocker | Gate suite tests `HEAD` while review covers uncommitted changes; no full re-run after fixes; Gate 0/D review only committed diffs | Fixed: one gate SHA for suite, both reviews and owner approval; full re-run on each new SHA (§2.4, P0-G-T4, PD-T03, OQ-X7). *Historical: v0.2 also allowed a snapshot commit; removed by D-P0-07, current procedure per round 3 below.* |
| 2 | blocker | Runner refuses every path under the real home, but profiles live in the repo under home | Fixed: refuse overlap with real agent/Xaibo roots and anything outside `$P`; symlink and repo-under-home tests (P0-T05) |
| 3 | blocker | Guard lists real session stores; owner audit reads real stores | Fixed per D-P0-04: guard has no store listing; audit is the named owner-run exception (P0-T04, P0-S9-T3, AGENTS.md) |
| 4 | major | Runner sets one agent variable, no `XAIBO_DATA_DIR` | Fixed: all five variables in every profile, tested (P0-T05, §3.2) |
| 5 | major | Guard misses deletions/removals; "volatile" hides changes; post-run only | Fixed: add/modify/delete/replace/truncate and keychain removal detected and tested; stated as detection only, prevention by runner + sandbox (P0-T04) |
| 6 | major | "Zero-write probe" not proven read-only; keychain checked after login | Fixed: credential backend proven isolated from docs before any probe; probe guarded; else UNVERIFIED (P0-T06) |
| 7 | major | Phase 0 privacy check misses ports, non-UUID IDs, most secrets; not run on the whole diff | Fixed: full D-20 pattern set from Phase 0, canary tests, whole gate diff via `--stdin`, manual review of reduced evidence (P0-T08) |
| 8 | major | Guard prints names; cleanup kills by `pgrep -f` substring | Fixed: category counts only; cleanup by recorded PID + start time (P0-T04, P0-T05, P0-T09) |
| 9 | major | P0-T03 packages the S3 SEA binary before P0-S3-T5 builds it | Fixed: entry added in P0-S3-T5 |
| 10 | major | electron-builder Phase 0 vs PRD App. C Phase 1 | Fixed per D-P0-05: PRD errata |
| 11 | major | TSX units have no harness-observable RED | Fixed: node:test RED on each unit's `.ts` logic before any `.tsx`; proved on the first Phase 1 `.tsx` unit (§3.6, P4-T03, OQ-X8) |
| 12 | major | Gate D contrast target undefined | Fixed: pair inventory + OQ-X15 thresholds (WCAG 2.2 AA default), per-pair report (PD-T01/T03, Gate D checklist) |
| 13 | major | EC-15 oversized record may stay unreadable (FR-08) | Fixed: bounded streaming extraction of readable content; gap only for unreadable bytes (P1-T14, OQ-P1-06, R-P1-09) |
| 14 | major | M2 lacks usage/context/cost receipt paths | Fixed: per-path M2 measurement incl. during import (Phase 3 §3.5) |
| 15 | major | (merged into #1: Gate 0/D diff omits post-commit changes) | Fixed with #1 |
| 16 | major | (merged into #1: no full re-run after fixes) | Fixed with #1 |
| 17 | minor | Report used before P0-T10 creates it | Fixed: P0-T10 first (§0.15, P0-T01 deps) |
| 18 | minor | Progress file: OQ-X2 shown pending; stale "(no entries yet)" | Fixed in this file |
| 19 | minor | EC-102 tests lack per-turn attribution and timestamp checks | Fixed (P4-T08) |

**Fix-delta re-review 1 (Codex).** *Historical: the R1 and R2 mechanics below (snapshot commit, record in progress file) were superseded by D-P0-07 and rounds 2–3.* Of the 19: 12 resolved, 7 partially resolved (#1, #2, #5, #6, #15, #16, #17). Verdict "no". New findings and dispositions:

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | blocker | Snapshot command can leak `GIT_INDEX_FILE` on failure; ignored tracked files can drop out | Fixed: subshell with `set -eu`, trap cleanup, index under `.git/`, `read-tree HEAD` seed, before/after check of branch and real index (§2.4 step 1) |
| R2 | blocker | Gate SHA recorded inside files that are part of that SHA; verdicts written after freeze | Fixed: gate SHA only in `.handoff/` during the gate; verdicts, dispositions and approval go in a separate gate-record commit checked by `git diff --stat` (§2.4 steps 1 and 8, P0-G-T4, PD-T05) |

## 9b. Claude second review record (2026-11-09, plan v0.2 → v0.3)

Diff `master` → working tree. 43 of 48 findings confirmed by a skeptic pass; duplicates merged below. Refuted (5): repo-wide tools reading in-repo temp credentials (graphify honours `.gitignore`); missing owner touchpoints (each task names its owner action); progress showing gate not passed after merge (by design, D-P0-07); P0-G-T5 "commit" wording (consistent with step 6); a "dead" `/logout` rule (used at retirement).

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition in v0.3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | major | Runner's `sandbox-exec` wrapper nests Seatbelt around Codex's tool sandbox and Electron helpers; no rule for when to wrap; deny tests cannot run inside the Claude sandbox | Runner always runs unsandboxed and always wraps; nesting probe on the Mac first; exceptions per new OQ-X16 (owner approval); real-sandbox tests in `spike:test:unsandboxed` (P0-T04, P0-T05, entry criterion 4) |
| C2 | major | Keychain items discovered once then polled by name: new names or second accounts invisible | Every snapshot enumerates attributes via `dump-keychain`; tests for new name and second account (P0-T04, OQ-X3) |
| C3 | major | Gate `npm ci` / `dist:mac` download Electron into `~/Library/Caches` | Absolute repo cache env in §2.4 step 1; `dist:mac` defaults only; `~/Library/Caches` before/after check |
| C4 | major | Opaque-ID rule flags ordinary identifiers and lockfile integrity; two scanners with drifting patterns | One scanner `scripts/privacy-scan.ts` from P0-T08 (P1-T02 extends, P1-T03 moves the list to `src/shared`); narrowed rule; lockfile excluded from diff scan; negative tests |
| C5 | minor | Write-only deny lets a mis-isolated CLI read real stores | Profile denies `file-read*` and `file-write*`; read test |
| C6 | minor | AGENTS.md "one exception" narrower than PRD §8 and the plan's later owner real-data steps; no Gate D exception | AGENTS.md reworded (stores defined; named owner-started sessions; guard reads named); Gate D exception added |
| C7 | minor | FR-45 `session_id` added by plan default only | Added to the P0-G-T2 PRD delta; P3-T14 captures it only after approval |
| C8 | minor | Stale status (branch "no commits", review pending, per-phase row) | Fixed (plan §0.1, header; progress §1/§2) |
| C9 | minor | One-time unsandboxed companion approval treated as standing | §2.4, §3.5, Phase 1 entry: per PRD App. B item 6 and OQ-X2 |
| C10 | minor | Gate SHA file deleted before step 8 needs it; fixed worktree path breaks re-runs; pre-freeze run undefined; P0-G-T4 a drifting copy of §2.4 | §2.4 restructured: step 0 pre-freeze, `mktemp` worktree removed after each run, SHA file deleted last; P0-G-T4 now a pointer plus Gate 0 additions |
| C11 | minor | Controlling Claude session's token refresh confounds the keychain check | P0-T06 steps 2–5 with no real session of that agent running; Claude steps owner-run with Claude Code exited; any change = stop |
| C12 | minor | PID recording vs `exec`; children missed | Own process group via `perl setpgrp`; leader record before exec; cleanup signals the group (P0-T05, P0-T09) |

| C14 | minor | Env test fails on shell-added variables | Uses `/usr/bin/env` directly (P0-T05) |

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| W1 | major | Supervisor inside its own process group cannot wait for the group to empty | Supervisor stays outside the child's group (child calls `setpgrp`); TERM then KILL after 5 s; tests for normal exit and cleanup-triggered shutdown (P0-T05) |
| W2 | major | Changes to existing keychain items with unrecognised names were ignored | Any non-agent add/modify/remove → `unmatched-change`, pause for owner; owner may mark a hashed service non-agent; compare rules reconciled; tests (P0-T04) |

**Codex re-review of W1–W3.** W3 resolved; W1, W2 partially. Dispositions:

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| X1 | major | Supervisor startup/shutdown races; group ID reuse; grandchildren cannot be reaped | Requirement stated; handshake over a pipe, TERM-ignoring sentinel keeps the group ID alive until signalling ends, fail closed on setup errors; waits for disappearance, does not reap; five race tests (P0-T05) |
| X2 | major | Keychain outcomes for mixed changes inconsistent across rules, tests and P0-T06 | Precedence defined (DIFF > unmatched-change > MATCH/expected-new); tests for expected+agent and expected+unrecognised; P0-T06 step 5 aligned (P0-T04, P0-T06) |

**Codex re-review of X1–X2.** X2 resolved; X1 partially. Dispositions:

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Y1 | major | Group kill cannot stop a descendant that leaves the group or session | Guarantee limited to the group; escapers detected via a per-run `XAIBO_RUN_TOKEN` environment marker after shutdown, reported as `survivors <n>`, fail closed; `setsid` test (P0-T05) |
| Y2 | major | Handshake failure after the sentinel starts leaves it running | Handshake now precedes the sentinel; post-handshake failures kill the known group; tests force failure before and after (P0-T05) |
| Y3 | minor | P0-T09 still said "reaps" | Fixed |

**Codex re-review of Y1–Y3.** Y2, Y3 resolved; Y1 partially. Instead of patching the supervisor again, it was simplified to standard job control:

| # | Sev | Finding (short) | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Z1 | major | Token scan misses an escaped descendant that clears its environment | Limit stated explicitly; backstop: processes started during the block and absent before are listed locally and pause live work for the owner; tests for both escape cases with before/after states (P0-T05) |
| Z2 | major | Group ID could be reused between acknowledgement and sentinel start | Sentinel and handshake removed: parent and child both `setpgid`; the leader is not reaped until group signalling ends, so its ID stays reserved (P0-T05) |
| Z3 | minor | "No process remains" contradicted the escaped-survivor case | Expected states before and after test cleanup stated |

**Focused Codex check of Z1–Z3.** Z1, Z3 resolved; Z2 partially. **Codex verdict: "yes with fixes"** for owner approval. The one fix (applied): the parent's `setpgid` returning `EACCES` after the child has exec'd is the expected macOS race; the parent verifies the child's group ID and continues; genuine setup failures stop the group before reaping; test added (P0-T05).

## Update rule

- Update in the same working change as each task, as soon as it becomes done or blocked; on every design decision where the PRD left a detail open; on every owner decision; and before each gate review (the review diff includes this file). Never batch updates at phase end.
- Spike findings only in `phase0_spikes.md`; this file links to them.
- Repo privacy rules apply in full: no counts, paths, IDs, ports or prompts from the owner's machine.
